2026-10-17T23:22:26.4275519Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Wait for router readiness before the sanity chat and rerun A2 on A1's image

A2 attempt 1 (Actions 39074570813, commit c272f7e, Slurm 24418) failed
before AIPerf. All four workers became healthy and the router answered
/health at 23:17:42, but the sanity chat sent one second later got 503:
pd_router "No prefill workers available". The router registers its
workers through an asynchronous workflow ("local_worker_registration"
started at 23:17:42), and /health in sglang-router 0.3.2 is a plain
liveness handler that returns 200 before any worker is registered. The
sanity chat (added with the placement change, a61d08d) is the first
request after READY; curl --fail under set -e then ended the job. A1 and
the placement run passed only because registration happened to finish
first.

Fix: the router wait in sglang.sbatch polls /readiness instead of
/health. In the pinned router source (sglang_router-0.3.2.tar.gz sha256
bdbea1d5..., src/server.rs) /readiness returns 200 in PD mode only when
at least one healthy Prefill and one healthy Decode worker are
registered, and 503 otherwise, which wait_http already retries. This
only moves READY by the registration time; the scored AIPerf phases and
every serving setting are unchanged. Runtime-only change, so the run
reuses A1's image (7c6286e5..., pinned in the config by c272f7e).

Checks: bash -n, the line-continuation check and the full pytest suite
pass.
